\textbf{\zh{复\zhglue 现\zhglue 命\zhglue 令\zhglue （\zhglue 换\zhglue 机\zhglue 器\zhglue 照\zhglue 抄\zhglue ）}}\zh{：}

\begin{lstlisting}[language=bash]
